NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Tuesday, September 11, 2018
ONE OR MORE SUPERVISORS MAY ATTEND VIA VIDEO AND/OR TELEPHONICALLY 
The Navajo County Board of Supervisors will hold a meeting open to the public on Tuesday, September 11, 2018, in the Supervisors’
Chamber in the Navajo County Governmental Complex, Holbrook, Arizona. The Board may vote to meet in a closed executive
session to discuss certain matters as indicated on the following agenda. The Board may meet in executive session pursuant to
A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (3) for legal advice on any item.
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of the Board’s office
at (928) 524-4053; at least 48 hours prior to the meeting so an accommodation can be arranged.
Items on the agenda may be considered out of order at the Chairman’s discretion. A copy of the agenda background material
provided to the Board, with the exception of items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection at Navajo
County website, www.navajocountyaz.gov 
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation
           
1. CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Individuals may address the Board on any relevant issue for up to 5
minutes. At the close of the call to the public, Board members may not respond to any comments
but may respond to criticism, ask staff to review a matter or ask that a matter be placed on a
future agenda.
 
 
 
2. CONSENT AGENDA: These items are considered to be routine or administrative in nature and
will be approved in a single motion. A Board member may request for any item to be removed
from the consent agenda and considered on the regular agenda:
 
 
a. Minutes: August 28, 2018 Regular meeting and September 6 Special meeting  
 
b. Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 August, 2018  
 
c. Clerk of Superior Court Report August 2018  
 
d. Juvenile Probation Report August 2018  
 
e. Constable Reports: Winslow Precinct #2 July; Show Low Precinct #5, August; and
Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, August 2018
 
 
f. Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1; Winslow Precinct #2; Snowflake Precinct #3;
Kayenta Precinct #4; Show Low Precinct #5; and Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6 August 2018
 
 
g. Personnel Actions  
 
h. Sympathy letters to Susan Berry and Debra Kester  
 
i. Contracts signed by County Manager/Assistant County Manager pursuant to Board of

<!-- page 2 -->
i. Contracts signed by County Manager/Assistant County Manager pursuant to Board of
Supervisors Resolution: Amendment Number Two, Jon Saline and Navajo County for
Professional Services as a Special Deputy Legal Defender
 
 
j. Approval of 5 Back Tax Land Parcels sold over the counter in the amount of $1,533.44  
 
k. Contract #DI19-002204 executed by Arizona Department of Economic Security in the amount
of $1,006,048 to provide WIOA Title 1 services throughout Navajo, Gila, and Apache Counties
 
 
l. Amendment of the Intergovernmental Agreement between Navajo County Recorder and the City
of Holbrook to extend the duration of the agreement to December 1, 2018
 
 
m. Resolution Number 16-18, canceling the election for the Heber/Overgaard Fire District, Joseph
City Fire District, Clay Springs – Pinedale Fire District, McLaws Road Fire District, Woodruff
Fire District, and Sun Valley Fire District and appointing the candidate(s) or deeming the
position vacant
 
 
n. Resolution Number 17-18, canceling the Nov. 8, 2016 Election for the Joseph City DWID,
Heber DWID, Ponderosa DWID, Overgaard Townsite DWID, Wonderland Acres DWID,
Woodruff DWID, Clay Springs DWID, Misty Mountain DWID  and Three-0-Three DWID and
appointing the candidate(s) or deeming the position vacant
 
 
o. Resolution Number 18-18, canceling the election for the Joseph City Sanitary District, Heber
Overgaard Sanitary District, and Pinetop-Lakeside Sanitary District and appointing the
candidate(s) or deeming the position vacant
 
 
p. Resolution Number 19-18, canceling the election for the NPC Community College District
Governing Board, District #3 and appointing the candidate(s) or deeming the position vacant
 
 
q. Resolution Number 20-18, canceling the election for the Navapache Hospital District and
appointing the candidate(s) or deeming the position vacant
 
 
r. Approve hiring outside counsel, Roberta Livesay, to represent Navajo County in Solar City et. al
v. Arizona Department of Revenue et al. in CV2018000905, regarding tax year 2017
 
 
s. Reappointment of (5) Workforce Development Board Members for Additional 3-year Terms  
 
3. ADMINISTRATIVE SERVICES:  
 
a. Navajo County Recognizes retiring employee, Ted Mathias (Jail Operations) for over 7 years of
service.
 
Presented By: Eric Scott
 
4. RECORDER:  
 
a. Presentation and discussion of the August 28, 2018 Primary Election.  
Presented By: Doris Clark/Rayleen Richards

<!-- page 3 -->
5. NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors
Session: 
 
 
a. REGULAR AGENDA:  
 
1. Consideration and approval of a proclamation declaring the month of September "National
Preparedness Month".
 
Presented By: Jeff Lee / Catrina Jenkins
 
6. PUBLIC WORKS:  
 
a. Consideration of award of Contract #B18-07-017 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material to
Perkins Cinders, Inc., Brimhall Sand, Rock & Building Materials, Inc., and Hatch Construction
& Paving, Inc.
 
Presented By: John Osgood
 
b. Consideration of award of contract #B18-07-019 Shilo Trail Drainage Improvement to Perkins
Cinders, Inc.
 
Presented By: John Osgood
 
7. COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from County Manager, County Attorney and Board
members.
 
 
 
8. ADJOURN  
 
9. AACO DISCUSSION AND PRESENTATION: Pursuant to ARS § 38-431.02 and ARS §
38-431 (3), notice is hereby given to the members of the Navajo County Board of Supervisors
and to the General Public that a quorum of the Board of Supervisors may be present for a
discussion and presentation with Arizona Association of Counties at the Chevelon Canyon
Conference Room 100 W. Public Works Drive in Holbrook, Arizona where no official action
will be taken. 
 
 
Dated: September 7, 2018 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
Posted: 9/7/18 @10:07 a.m. By: mb                         
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.k.        
Meeting Date: 09/11/2018  
Title: Contract #DI19-002204
Submitted By: Carla Fails, Operations Manager
Department: Northeastern Az Innovative Workforce
Motion before the Board:
Contract #DI19-002204 executed by Arizona Department of Economic Security in the amount of
$1,006,048 to provide WIOA Title 1 services throughout Navajo, Gila, and Apache Counties
Background:
The purpose of this agreement is to provide Workforce Innovation and Opportunity Act (WIOA)
Title I services to eligible Adults, Dislocated Workers, and Youth throughout the designated Local
Workforce Development Area (LWDA)  These services will be provided in accordance with
Federal and State regulations and the most current Local Plan. 
The total $ amount for Contract #DI19-002204 is $1,006,048.   The term of this Agreement shall
begin on July 1, 2018 and shall end on June 30, 2023, unless otherwise amended. 
Fiscal Impact
Fiscal Year: PY18/FY19
Budgeted Y/N: Y
Amount Requested: 0
Fiscal Impact:
N/A  WIOA is a federally funded program.

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.m.        
Meeting Date: 09/11/2018  
Title: Fire Dist. C&A Nov. 6, 2018
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Motion before the Board:
Resolution Number 16-18, canceling the election for the Heber/Overgaard Fire District, Joseph
City Fire District, Clay Springs – Pinedale Fire District, McLaws Road Fire District, Woodruff
Fire District, and Sun Valley Fire District and appointing the candidate(s) or deeming the position
vacant
Background:
48-802. Election procedures
 
(D) 4. Except for an election to reorganize a fire district, nominating petitions shall be filed with
the board of supervisors as prescribed by title 16, chapter 3. If only one person files or no person
files a nominating petition for an election to fill a position on the district board or the position of
elected fire chief or elected secretary-treasurer for which the term of office is to expire, the board
of supervisors may cancel the election for that position and appoint the person who filed the
nominating petition to fill the position. If no person files a nominating petition for an election to
fill a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for those offices and those
offices are deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is
appointed pursuant to this paragraph is fully vested with the powers and duties of the office as if
elected to that office.

RESOLUTION NO ______ - 18 
 
A RESOLUTION OF THE NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS, CANCELLING THE ELECTIONS FOR 
THE HEBER/OVERGAARD FIRE DIST., JOSEPH CITY FIRE DIST., CLAY SPRINGS – PINEDALE FIRE DIST., 
MCLAWS RD. FIRE DIST., WOODRUFF FIRE DIST., SUN VALLEY FIRE DIST., PINETOP FIRE DIST; AND 
APPOINTING THE CANDIDATE(S) OR DEEMING THE POSITION VACANT, PURSUANT TO ARS § 48-802 (D)(4) 
 
 
 
WHEREAS, ARS § 48-802 (D)(4) provides as follows with respect to Fire District Board of Directors 
positions : 
 
“Except for an election to reorganize a fire district, nominating petitions shall be filed with the board of 
supervisors as prescribed by title 16, chapter 3. If only one pers on files or no person files a nominating petition for an 
election to fill a position on the district board or the position of elected fire chief or elected secretary -treasurer for 
which the term of office is to expire, the board of supervisors may cancel t he election for that position and appoint the 
person who filed the nominating petition to fill the position. If no person files a nominating petition for an election to fill 
a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for those offi ces and those offices are deemed 
vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is appointed pursuant to this paragraph is fully 
vested with the powers and duties of the office as if elected to that office. ” 
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, A.R.S. § 16-410 (A) authorizes the Board to take such action no earlier than seventy -five (75) 
days before the election, and it is now less than seventy-five (75) days before the November 6, 2018 General 
Election at which the Fire Districts Board of Directors will be elected;  
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, the officer in charge of elections, has certified that the number of persons who filed nominating 
petitions to fill the Fire District Board of Directors positions is less than or equal to the number of positions  available, 
attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A”  
 
NOW, THEREFORE BE IT RESOLVED by the Board of Supervisors as follows:  
 
That the elections for the HEBER/OVERGAARD FIRE DIST., JOSEPH CITY FIRE DIST., CLAY SPRINGS 
– PINEDALE FIRE DIST., MCLAWS RD. FIRE DIST., WOODRUFF FIRE DIST., SUN VALLEY FIRE DIST., 
PINETOP FIRE DIST. Board Member positions , attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A”, are hereby 
cancelled by the Navajo County Board of Supervisors.

<!-- page 416 -->
PASSED, APPROVED AND ADOPTED at Holbrook, Arizona on September 11, 2018 
 
  
 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
 
 
                
 By ________________________________________ 
 Steve Williams 
 Chairwoman of the Board

Title: Sanitary C&A Nov. 6, 2016
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director

Motion before the Board:
Resolution Number 18-18, canceling the election for the Joseph City Sanitary District,
Heber Overgaard Sanitary District, and Pinetop-Lakeside Sanitary District and appointing
the candidate(s) or deeming the position vacant
Background:
48-2010. Board of directors; qualifications; term; appointment; election; conversion;
reorganization; compensation; expenses
 
A. …Except for an election to reorganize a sanitary district, candidates for directors shall file
nominating petitions with the board of supervisors as prescribed by title 16, chapter 3. If only one
person files or no person files a nominating petition for an election to fill a district office, the
board of supervisors may cancel the election for the position and appoint the person who filed the
nominating petition to fill the position. If no person files a nominating petition for an election to
fill a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for that office and that office
is deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is appointed
pursuant to this section is fully vested with the powers and duties of the office as if elected to that
office.

WHEREAS, ARS § 48-2010 (A) provides as follows with respect to Sanitary District Board of Directors 
positions : 
 
“…Except for an election to reorganize a sanitary district, candidates for directors shall file nominating 
petitions with the board of supervisors as prescribed by title 16, chapter 3. If only one person files or no person files a 
nominating petition for an election to fill a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for the 
position and appoint the person who filed the nominating petition to fill the position. If no person files a nominating 
petition for an election to fill a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for that office and that 
office is deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is appointed pursuant to this 
section is fully vested with the powers and duties of the office as if elected to that office.” 
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, A.R.S. § 16-410 (A) authorizes the Board to take such action no earlier than seventy -five (75) 
days before the election, and it is now less than seventy-five (75) days before the November 6, 2108 General 
Election at which the Sanitary Districts Board of Directors will be elected;  
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, the officer in charge of elections, has certified that the number of persons who filed nominating 
petitions to fill the Sanitary Districts Board of Directors positions is less than or equal to the number of positions 
available, attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A”; 
 
 
 
 
NOW, THEREFORE BE IT RESOLVED by the Board of Supervisors as follows:  
 
That the elections for the Heber/Overaard  Sanitary Dist., and Joseph City Sanitary Dist. And Pinetop-

<!-- page 427 -->
Lakeside Sanitary Dist. Board of Directors positions , attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A” are 
hereby cancelled and deemed elected/vacant by the Navajo County Board of Supervisors.

Background:
48-1908. Board of directors; members; qualifications; terms; organization
 
C. Candidates for a district board office shall file nominating petitions with the board of
supervisors as prescribed by title 16, chapter 3. If only one person files or no person files a
nominating petition for an election to fill a position on the board for which the term of office is to
expire, then the board may cancel the election for that position and appoint the person who filed a
nominating petition to fill the position. If no person files a nominating petition for an election to
fill a district office, the board of supervisors may cancel the election for that office and that office
is deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. Vacancies occurring other than
by expiration of term may be filled by the remaining directors, except that if the remaining
directors do not constitute a quorum, the county board of supervisors shall make the appointment
to fill the vacancy.

Title: Approval of Outside Counsel For Solar City Tax Appeal (TY 2017)
Submitted For: Jason Moore, Deputy County Attorney 
Submitted By: Jason Moore, Deputy County Attorney
Department: County Attorney
Motion before the Board:
Approve hiring outside counsel, Roberta Livesay, to represent Navajo County in Solar City et. al
v. Arizona Department of Revenue et al. in CV2018000905, regarding tax year 2017
Background:
Navajo County has been named as a Defendant in a new lawsuit by Solar City Corporation and
other solar companies regarding property that was centrally assessed by the Arizona Department
of Revenue.  The Board has already approved Ms. Livesay representing Navajo County in the
litigation for 2015 and 2016, and the issues presented to the Court in the latest lawsuit are the
same as tax years 2015 and 2016.   Navajo County and twelve other counties have a cost sharing
agreement in place for Ms. Livesay's services, and this case will be subject to the same agreement.

Board of Supervisors Regular 5.a.1.        
Meeting Date: 09/11/2018  
Title: 2018 National Preparedness Month 
Submitted For: Catrina Jenkins, EMP Director 
Submitted By: Catrina Jenkins, EMP Director
Department: NC Public Health Services District Division: Emergency Management
Presented By: Jeff Lee / Catrina Jenkins
Motion before the Board:
Consideration and approval of a proclamation declaring the month of September "National
Preparedness Month".
Background:
National Preparedness Month, recognized each September, provides an opportunity to remind us
that we all must prepare ourselves and our families now and throughout the year. This NPM will
focus on planning, with an overarching theme: Disasters Happen. Prepare Now. Learn How.  
Disasters such as wildfires and floods, reminded our communities of the importance of preparing
for emergencies.  Often, we will be the first ones in our communities to take action after a disaster
strikes and before first responders arrive, so it is important to prepare in advance to help yourself,
your family and your community.
Each week we will share important information for our citizens to use in making sure they are
ready for the hazards we face in our region. 
Week 1: Sept 1-8                          Make and Practice Your Plan
Week 2: Sept 9-15*                      Learn Life Saving Skills
Week 3: Sept 16-22                      Check Your Insurance Coverage
Week 4: Sept 23-29                      Save For an Emergency
September 15 is a National Day of Action
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval.

Preparedness Month Proclamation

<!-- page 472 -->
Navajo County               
Proclamation 
 
Navajo County - For Official Use Only 
  National Preparedness Month 
WHEREAS, every year September is designated as National Preparedness Month; and 
WHEREAS, the theme for National Preparedness Month 2018 is “Disasters Happen. Prepare 
Now. Learn How.”; and 
WHEREAS, the primary goal for Navajo County Emergency Management and Preparedness is to 
form a more resilient community through education, training and exercises, and planning for any 
and all types of disasters; and 
WHEREAS, the weekly themes this year include information on how to Make and Practice Your 
Plan, Learning Life-Saving Skills, Check Your Coverage and Save For an Emergency; and 
WHEREAS, throughout the year, Navajo County Emergency Management continues promote 
preparedness efforts in the communities throughout Navajo County, the region as well as the 
state. Navajo County Emergency Management will continue to strengthen our enduring 
partnerships, forge new collaborations and seek new and innovative ways to bring preparedness 
to the “Whole Community”; and 
WHEREAS, The Board of Supervisors would like to officially recognize the hard work and 
dedication of all those involved in ensuring the safety of our citizens and their property, 
businesses and communities, including first responders, volunteers, non-governmental agencies 
and most of all the citizens themselves. 
NOW, THEREFORE, BE IT PROCLAIMED, by the Board of Supervisors of Navajo County and the 
Sheriff of Navajo County do hereby proclaim the month of September, as “National 
Preparedness Month” in Navajo County, Arizona. 
PASSED, ADOPTED AND APPROVED by the Board of Supervisors on September 11, 2018. 
          
____________________________ 
Steve Williams, Chairman 
 
ATTEST: 
_________________________________ 
Melissa W. Buckley, Clerk of the Board

Title: Award Contract #B18-07-017 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material 
Submitted For: John Osgood, Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Cheryl Hollins, Account Specialist
Department: Public Works
Presented By: John Osgood
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of award of Contract #B18-07-017 Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material to
Perkins Cinders, Inc., Brimhall Sand, Rock & Building Materials, Inc., and Hatch Construction &
Paving, Inc.
Background:
This is a eighteen-month contract for the Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material.  Sealed
proposals were received by the Board of Supervisors at the Navajo County Government Center,
Holbrook, Arizona until 3:00 p.m. M.S.T., August 22, 2018 in strict accordance with
specifications on file in the office of the Navajo County Department of Public Works.  No bids
were accepted after 3:00 p.m. M.S.T.  The bids were publicly opened and read aloud at 3:15 p.m.
Navajo County may award contracts to all bidders for each item, and select the bidder at the time
of purchase based on price, convenience, availability, and/or quality.  Three bids were received
and the award of contracts to all bidders allows adequate coverage for materials by location.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends approval.

THIS CONTRACT  is made and entered into as of September 26, 2018 (the 
“Effective Date”) by and between Navajo County (“County”) and  Perkins Cinders, Inc.

1. SCOPE OF WORK : Contractor shall furnish any and all materials , equipment and 
labor required for performing all work for Supply of Aggregate for Cover Material , 
Contract, # B18-07-017, in acco rdance with the Contract Documents , in a 
workmanlike manner to the satisfaction of County through its Engineers. 
 
2. CONTRACT DOCUMENTS: The Contract Documents comprise this Contract and 
the attached ☒ Call for Bids, ☐ Specifications, ☐ General Provisions, ☒ Special 
Provisions, ☒ Proposal dated  August 17, 2018 ☒ Bidding Schedule, ☒ Bid 
Bond, ☐ Performance Bond, ☒ No Collusion Affidavit, ☒ Subcontracting 
Certification, ☐ Plans, and addenda thereto, if any.  If there is any conflict in the 
documents, this Contract shall control over all others; the Specifications shall control 
over the General Provisions and S pecial Provisions; and the Special Provisions shall 
control over the General Provisions. 
 
3. NO LIENS / TIME FOR COMPLETION: Contractor shall furnish all materials, 
equipment and labor in accordance with the Contract Documents free and clear of 
all claims, liens  and charges whatsoever, within the time, or times, stated in the 
Proposal. 
 
4. CANCELLATION: This Contract is subject to cancellation pursuant to A.R.S. §  38-
511 (concerning conflicts of interest).

awarded items in the Bidding Schedule for Contract #B18-07-017.  Contractor shall 
submit invoices to County f or all work performed hereunder .  No later than 30 days 
after each invoice has been verified and approved by the Engineer, County shall pay 
to Contractor 100% of the approved amount.

compliance with all federal immigration laws and regulations relating to their

deemed a material breach of the Contract and may result in ter mination of the

for random inspection by County during normal business hours.

<!-- page 482 -->
Board of Supervisors Regular 6.b.        
Meeting Date: 09/11/2018  
Title: Shilo Trail Drainage Improvements
Submitted For: John Osgood, Public Works Director 
Submitted By: Jeanine Carruthers, Deputy Director Administration
Department: Public Works
Presented By: John Osgood
Motion before the Board:
Consideration of award of contract #B18-07-019 Shilo Trail Drainage Improvement to Perkins
Cinders, Inc.
Background:
The Shilo Trail drainage improvement project represents a collaborative effort between
neighborhood residents and Navajo County staff to improve drainage conditions along a segment
of the Shilo Trail roadway within the Linden Trails Subdivision.  As a result of several
neighborhood meetings, engineering analysis, and staff discussions around
construction solutions, the drainage improvement project consists of culvert and
driveway demolition and replacement, installation of box culverts, grading, paving,
and other incidental drainage work. Sealed proposals were received by the Board
of Directors office at the Navajo County Governmental Center, Holbrook, Arizona until 3:00 p.m.
M.S.T, August 29, 2018, in strict accordance with the specifications on file in the office of the
Navajo County Public Works.  No bids were accepted after 3:00 p.m. M.S.T.  The bids were
publicly opened and read aloud at 3:15 p.m.  Three bids were received and Perkins Cinders, Inc. is
the lowest responsible and responsive bidder.  This bid was solicited under the Navajo County
Flood Control District and after further review, this is a HURF eligible project, therefore; staff is
requesting that the Board award the contract to Perkins Cinders, Inc. under the Navajo County
Public Works department.  Perkins Cinders, Inc. has been contacted and concurs with this change.
Recommendation: 
Staff recommends award of contract B18-07-019 Shilo Trail Drainage Improvement to Perkins
Cinders, Inc.
Fiscal Impact
Fiscal Year: 2019
Budgeted Y/N: Y
Amount Requested: 80832.23
Fiscal Impact:
